Final run: 9,000 cases, 7,262 data-read comparisons and 713,291 op outputs (168,790 ops, 19,685 adds, 7,683 removes, 42,747 exits, 3,308 xfers, 5,512 updates, 598,807 effect lines), 0 mismatches, 1,738 excluded (upstream-undefined). Planted bugs `L9_PLANT` 1411-1432: 20 of 22 caught. 1411 (a zero-slot rider replaced by its first passenger) is unobservable without a `ParachuteContain` (**CORRECTED 2026-10-22, batch R8**: now caught, the scene has parachutes), and 1424 (the payload added) is unobservable with the test's factory, which makes nothing (`newobject` is a line of the log). Three plants survived the first run and were made visible: 1426, because the driver's body had InactiveBody's health of 0 and a heal that did nothing (it now has the scene's health and logs `heal`, as the Rust probe does with `probe_effect_trace`); 1430, because op 25 only ever asked `isPassengerAllowedToFire(INVALID_ID)`; and 1413, because own-player riders were too rare (one object in two now belongs to player 0). The 37 batch-R plants were run again on the widened generator and are all still caught. **Design:** trait `TransportContainVirtuals: OpenContainVirtuals` (`tc`, `tc_mut`, `is_specific_rider_free_to_exit`, `create_payload`), with `tc_*` generic free functions for the derived transports. **New cut points:** `ContainCall::{SetDisabled, ClearDisabled, NewObject}`, `ContainCutTables::{exit_bones, valid_movement_terrain}`, `World::{contain_set_disabled, contain_clear_disabled, contain_drawable_model_condition, contain_exit_bone_world_position, contain_valid_movement_terrain, contain_new_payload_object, weapon_slot_facts, weapon_transfer_next_shot_stats}`, `CutCall::WeaponTransferNextShotStats`, `ModuleCall::{PhysicsApplyMotiveForce, PhysicsGetCenterOfMassOffset, PhysicsSetPitchRate, AiSetAttitude, AiWakeUpAndAttemptToTarget, AiGetFreeToExit, AiHasCurLocomotor, ReserveDoorForExit, KillRidersWhoAreNotFreeToExit, IsSpecificRiderFreeToExit}`; `IniErrorKind::BadArg` (`ERROR_BAD_ARG`, `newObject(NULL)` in `createPayload`, compared as the op's throw status 3). **Open:** ~~`RailedTransportContain`~~ (batch R3), ~~`RiderChangeContain`~~ (batch R5), ~~`HelixContain`~~ (batch R4), ~~`OverlordContain`~~ (batch R6), ~~`InternetHackContain`~~ (batch R3) (on `TransportContainVirtuals`), then `GarrisonContain`, `TunnelContain`, `CaveContain`, ~~`HealContain`~~ (batch R7), ~~`ParachuteContain`~~ (batch R8), `MobNexusContain`.

**Layer-9 contain batch R3 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `InternetHackContain` (`modules/internet_hack_contain.rs`) and `RailedTransportContain` (`modules/railed_transport_contain.rs`), compiled unchanged in the contain driver; `RailedTransportDockUpdate.h` is kept out of the unit by defining its include guard and copying its one interface class verbatim. **Design:** `TransportContainVirtuals` gained a `t_*` hook for every `OpenContain` virtual that `TransportContain` or a derived class overrides; `transport_open_contain_virtuals!` writes the `OpenContainVirtuals` impl (for `TransportContain` too) routing each to its hook, and `derived_transport_contain_glue!` writes the construction, slot entry points, `UpdateBehavior` and `Snapshot` of a derived class with no state of its own, so a derived class's file holds only upstream's overrides. **Upstream fact:** `RailedTransportContain::exitObjectViaDoor` calls `getRailedTransportDockUpdateInterface()` unqualified; inside the member function that finds `BehaviorModule`'s virtual, which a contain returns `NULL` for, not the file's static helper, so it never unloads (the helper is dead code). **New cut points:** `ModuleCall::{DockIsOpen, DockSetOpen}` (the probe answers `dock_open` and traces `docksetopen`), `Object::getDockUpdateInterface` in the driver (verbatim). Results: 9,000 cases, 7,244 data-read comparisons and 713,673 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 1,756 excluded; plants 1441-1446 all caught; the 57 earlier plants rerun on the widened generator (results in the test header).

**Layer-9 contain batch R4 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `HelixContain` (`modules/helix_contain.rs`), compiled unchanged in the contain driver. Its data class lifts `TransportContainModuleData`'s three tables; the module has state (`m_portableStructureID`) and xfer version 2, so its glue is written out. New hooks on `TransportContainVirtuals` (`t_add_to_contain`, `t_add_to_contain_list`, `t_remove_from_contain`, `t_is_enclosing_container_for`, `t_friend_get_rider`, `t_redeploy_occupants`, `t_on_die`, `t_on_delete`, `t_on_object_created`, `t_on_body_damage_state_change`, `t_other_call`); `OpenContainVirtuals` gained `on_object_created` and `on_body_damage_state_change` (dispatched from `contain_module_call`), and `friend_get_rider` now reads the world. **New cut points:** `ContainCall::{SetTeam, SetDamageState, StealthReceiveGrant}`, `ModuleCall::StealthReceiveGrant`. **Test changes:** block `H`, class 4, the PORTABLE_STRUCTURE kind, ops 56 (`onBodyDamageStateChange`) and 57 (`clientVisibleContainedFlashAsSelected`), the weapon bonus word in each object's state (the driver's `clearWeaponBonusCondition` stand-in now clears the bit, as Object.cpp does), `onObjectCreated` called on the C++ side as `Object`'s constructor calls it, and an undefined setup excluded. **A test defect found and fixed:** the decode masked a fixed byte range (5..9) as the scheduling word of a save, which is right only for `OpenContain`; the derived classes passed by luck (the high bytes of a frame are zero), and a Helix load of another class's save read the unmasked word as version bytes (Rust: the registration frame; C++: 0). The C++ driver now runs `GameLogic::registerObject`'s loop over the containers (due now, frame 1 at frame 0), and the word is compared, not masked. Results: 9,000 cases, 6,985 data-read comparisons and 684,814 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 2,015 excluded; plants 1451-1462: 11 caught, 1452 unobservable (payload added; stub factory).

**Layer-9 contain batch R5 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `RiderChangeContain` (`modules/rider_change_contain.rs`). **Upstream facts:** the class declares its own private `m_extraSlotsInUse` and `m_frameExitNotBusy`, hiding `TransportContain`'s (its `getExtraSlotsInUse` answers its own, always 0); `xfer` writes `m_payloadCreated` a second time after the base's, then its own two; `m_scuttledOnFrame` is not saved; `ObjectStatusType` (the riders' status) is forward-declared and defined nowhere (MSVC sizes it as int; build.rs gives clang an `enum : int`). **New pieces:** `World::template_is_equivalent_to_object` (the rider match, `ThingTemplate::isEquivalentTo` on the factory's roots), `ContainCall::{ExperienceSetVeterancyLevel, ExperienceSetExperienceAndLevel, KillWith}`, `World::contain_clear_model_condition_flags`, `ModuleCall::AiChooseLocomotorSet`, client events `MessageCreateSelectedGroup`, `MessageRemoveFromSelectedGroup`, `UiSelectDrawable`, `UiDeselectDrawable`, `UiSetDisplayedMaxWarning`; `LOCOMOTOR_SET_NAMES`. **Test changes:** block `C`, class 5, scene rider sets, the local player (player 0) on both sides, each object's command set override and veterancy level in its state, the scene's templates are the factory's (same template ids), and the slow-death driver's `clearModelConditionFlags` stand-in clears every flag of the mask (it cleared only the first). Results: 9,000 cases, 6,960 data-read comparisons and 674,111 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 2,040 excluded; plants 1471-1479 all caught.

**Layer-9 contain batch R6 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `OverlordContain` (`modules/overlord_contain.rs`; `OverlordContain.cpp` compiled with its one `while( *it )` loop ended at `end()` by build.rs; its header includes `GameLOgic/GameLogic.h` with a capital O, which MSVC's file system ignores, so the module shim has a forwarding header at that path). **Design change:** upstream's base code asks `getContainCount()`, `getContainedItemsList()`, `getContainMax()`, `isDisplayedOnControlBar()` and `isKickOutOnCapture()` through the virtual, so an Overlord redirects them (`TransportContain::update` heals the bunker's passengers, for example); these `OpenContainVirtuals` methods now take `&World`, and another object's contain is read in its slot (`open_contain::view_*`, one macro over every contain class). **Audit found three translation defects from batches R/R4/R5,** invisible until a class overrode the list: `getContainedItemsMass`, `HelixContain::redeployOccupants` and `RiderChangeContain::friend_getRider` read `m_containList` directly upstream, but the Rust called the virtual list; fixed. **Ring of redirections:** two Overlords that carry each other recurse for ever upstream (the C++ reference looped in `getContainCount`, the tail call compiled into a jump, and hit the driver's alarm); the Rust detects it (a depth limit on the calls, and reaching the running module's empty slot through a read) and records `Undefined::EndlessLoop`. Results: 9,000 cases, 6,919 data-read comparisons and 655,505 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 2,081 excluded; plants 1491-1499: 7 caught as mismatches, 1498 as a crash (an endless recursion), 1491 unobservable (payload added; stub factory).

**Layer-9 contain batch R7 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `HealContain` (`modules/heal_contain.rs`), the first class derived directly from `OpenContain` (it implements `OpenContainVirtuals` itself: `update` and `isHealContain`; its data lifts `OpenContainModuleData`'s two tables). **Test changes:** block `L`, class 7, each object's contained-by frame (the driver's `Object` stand-in now keeps `m_containedByFrame`, set by `onContainedBy` as Object.cpp does), the query op's hash takes the class queries (`isHealContain`, `isTunnelContain`, `isRiderChangeContain`, `isSpecialOverlordStyleContainer`, `isSpecialZeroSlotContainer`, `isGarrisonable`, `isBustable`), and op 55 asks transports only (it cast any classed container to `TransportContain`, which crashed on a `HealContain`). Results: 9,000 cases, 6,873 data-read comparisons and 654,749 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 2,127 excluded; plants 1501-1505 all caught.

**Layer-9 contain batch R8 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `ParachuteContain` (`modules/parachute_contain.rs`), derived directly from `OpenContain` as `HealContain` is; the driver compiles `ParachuteContain.cpp` unchanged. **Test changes:** block `P`, class 8; ops 58 (`onCollide` with the ground), 59 (`setOverrideDestination`), 60 (`onDrawableBoundToObject`) and 61 (the held flag); per object the held flag, the parachute bones `PARA_COG` / `PARA_ATTCH` / `PARA_MAN`, the locomotor's sway (pitch and roll stiffness and damping) and the producer; per scene the gravity, the terrain's ground height, water and pathfinder cell type by tile, the skirmish AI players and `findPositionAround`'s answer. The logic generator is now seeded before the objects are made as well as after, on both sides (the constructor draws the two sway rates). **Harness corrections found on the way:** the driver's `Object::convertBonePosToWorldPos` was the identity; it now pushes the bone through the transform `Thing::setOrientation` builds from the object's position and angle, as `Thing.cpp` does, and the Rust `contain_exit_bone_world_position` converts through the object's real transform too (`ContainCutTables::exit_bones` became `pristine_bones`: the art's position, not the world's). `PhysicsBehavior::applyForce`'s stand-in (from the slow-death test) named the slow-death test's object; it now asks the contain test for the owner. **Upstream fact found:** a ring of special zero-slot containers (parachutes that hold each other) makes `Object::getTransportSlotCount` recurse until the stack is gone; `World::get_transport_slot_count` now records `Undefined::EndlessLoop` past 64 levels. **And a cost found by a plant:** with parachutes (the first non-enclosing container) the walk of `addOrRemoveObjFromWorld` reaches the riders' riders, and plant 1376 (the already-contained refusal dropped) lets a ring hold an object twice, so both walks branched at every level and ran to the depth limit on every branch (11 GB before the run was stopped). Both now stop the whole walk once the ring is found (`ContainCutTables::ring_found`, and a flag through `transport_slot_count_at`); the result is undefined either way, and the unplanted run is unchanged. Results: 9,000 cases, 6,709 data-read comparisons and 642,389 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 2,291 excluded; plants 1511-1533 all caught (1526 as a C++ stack overflow: without the Rust guard the C++ runs into the ring); the 97 earlier caught plants were run again and are all still caught, and 1411 (batch R2) is now caught too, so 120 in all; 1424, 1452 and 1491 remain unobservable here. **New cut points:** `ContainCall::{PartitionFindPositionAround, PathfindGetCell, DrawableSetInstanceMatrix}`, `ContainCutTables::{pristine_bones, find_position, cell_types, skirmish_ai_players}`, `World::{contain_drawable_pristine_bone, contain_convert_bone_pos_to_world_pos, contain_partition_find_position_around, contain_pathfind_cell_type, contain_player_is_skirmish_ai, contain_drawable_set_instance_matrix, get_object_exit_interface}`, `ModuleCall::{AiHunt, AiLocomotorSway, AiLocomotorSetUltraAccurate, AiLocomotorSetCloseEnoughDist, AiLocomotorSetCloseEnoughDist3D, PhysicsSetIsInFreeFall, UseSpawnRallyPoint}`, and the `OpenContainVirtuals::contain_react_to_transform_change` hook.

**Layer-9 contain batch R9 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `MobNexusContain` (`modules/mob_nexus_contain.rs`), derived from `OpenContain`; the driver compiles `MobNexusContain.cpp` with two mechanical edits made by `build.rs` (`MobNexusContain_edit.cpp`): `if( *it )` of an empty special zero-slot rider's list and update's `while( *it )` stop at `end()` (the Rust side records `Undefined::EndIteratorDereferenced`). **Test changes:** block `M`, class 9 (scene-built from the transport's scene fields, no payload), op 62 (`tryToEvacuate`, `ModuleCall::TryToEvacuate`), and the op group of batches R8 and R9 widened (capacity checks and adds; one op in four). **Upstream facts found:** (1) `MobNexusContain::isValidContainerFor` adds `getContainCount()` (`UnsignedInt`) into its sum, so its capacity test compares unsigned and a maximum of -1 takes anything; `TransportContain` converts the count to `Int` first and compares signed (checked: changing the transport to unsigned broke two cases); (2) the parachute's rider is `getContainList().front()` whenever `getContainCount()` is positive: a count and a list that disagree is undefined (it crashed the C++), now recorded. **A defect in the Xfer layer, found by this batch:** on a short read, `fread` has already copied the bytes there were into the value before it fails; `Xfer::xfer_real` and the other multi-byte primitives (`xfer_int`, `xfer_unsigned_int`, `xfer_short`, `xfer_unsigned_short`, `xfer_int64`, `xfer_color`, `xfer_object_id`, `xfer_drawable_id` and the kind of a kind-of mask) returned the error before writing them back, so a failed load left the value whole where upstream leaves its first bytes overwritten (a parachute's pitch, 0x3b7db8c8 against upstream's 0x3b7db800). They now write the bytes back, then return the error. **The mob scene:** one scene in eight is built so that a mob nexus meets a parachute that holds a 2-slot infantry, and a 3-slot rider already inside (plants 1543 and 1545 survived the random scenes); it found that `OverlordContain` indexed its list where upstream reads `front()` with the size and the list disagreeing (`onCapture`, `onBodyDamageStateChange`, `onDie`): a Rust panic, now recorded as undefined. **The crash trap** (`l9_crash_trap_m.inc`) now handles its signal on the alternate signal stack, so a C++ stack overflow that a planted Rust bug steers into (a ring of containers) is a status-99 mismatch, not a dead process. Results and plants: see the test header (`tests/differential_contain_open.rs`, batch R9).

**Layer-9 contain batch R10 (2026-10-22, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `TunnelContain` (`modules/tunnel_contain.rs`), derived from `OpenContain`, on the player's `TunnelTracker` (`world/tunnel_tracker.rs`, translated and tested in an earlier batch), which an operation that touches the world checks out of the player's slot. The driver compiles `TunnelTracker.cpp` and `TunnelContain.cpp` unchanged (the tracker renamed `ModTestTunnelTracker` by the module prelude: the tunnel tracker's own test links another copy); every scene player has a tracker, and the state written after each op ends with each player's tracker (list, size, tunnel ids, count, nemesis). **Test changes:** block `N` (`TimeForFullHeal`), class 10 (heal time the scene's heal frames), the template's occlusion delay, and `TheGlobalData->m_maxTunnelCapacity` per scene. **New cut points:** `ContainCall::{AcademyUnitEnteredTunnelNetwork, HandlePartitionCellMaintenance, SetSafeOcclusionFrame}` and the matching `World::contain_*`, `ContainCutTables::null_player_read` (a `&World` query that meets an object with no controlling player). **`TunnelTracker` under the contain test's ablation:** its `onContainedBy`, `onRemovedFrom` and `destroyObject` calls go through the contain cut points when `cuts.contain.stub_object_calls` is set (the tracker's own test, which leaves it off, is unchanged). Results: 9,000 cases, 6,477 data-read comparisons and 1,218,197 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 2,523 excluded; plants 1561-1572 all caught (1563 first planted on the tracker's `isInContainer` test, an equivalent mutant: the tracker's removal is a no-op for an absent rider; it was moved to the `onRemoving` call). The nemesis path of `update` is unobservable here (the test's body keeps no last damage).

**Layer-9 contain batch R11 (2026-10-23, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `CaveContain` (`modules/cave_contain.rs`), derived from `OpenContain`, on the cave system's trackers (`world/cave_system.rs`, `World::with_cave_tracker`). The driver compiles `CaveSystem.cpp` and `CaveContain.cpp` unchanged (renamed by the module prelude, as the tracker), with a cave system per case, a team factory stand-in (the scene's teams by number) and `defect`'s line naming the team. **Test changes:** block `V` (`CaveIndex`), class 11 (the scene's index), ops 63 (`onBuildComplete`), 64 (`tryToSetCaveIndex`), 65 (`recalcApparentControllingPlayer`); the create modules' `onCreate` after each object is made, as `ThingFactory::newObject` does; one case in two starts with every container built; the cave scene (one scene in eight that is not the mob scene: a cave takes a rider, then is saved and loaded); and each op's state ends with the cave system's slots. **Upstream facts kept:** a cave whose index has no tracker (not built, or never registered) reads `NULL` everywhere (recorded as undefined); `harmAndForceExitAllContained` and `killAllContained` are `OpenContain`'s, on the module's own list, which is always empty; `getApparentControllingPlayer` is `OpenContain`'s `NULL`, so the indicator colour is never set; a loaded original team the team factory does not know is `SC_INVALID_DATA` after `m_originalTeam` was set to `NULL` (the Rust module's xfer has no world, so `World::xfer_module_slot` asks `CaveContain::check_loaded_team` after the load). **Harness correction:** the driver's `onContainedBy` and `onRemovedFrom` stand-ins dereferenced a `NULL` container, which `Object.cpp` accepts (a tunnel caving in passes one): they are `NULL`-safe now. Results: 9,000 cases, 0 mismatches; plants 1581-1590 all caught (1581 by the cave scene).

**Layer-9 contain batch R12 (2026-10-23, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `GarrisonContain` (`modules/garrison_contain.rs`), derived from `OpenContain`. The driver compiles `GarrisonContain.cpp` with one mechanical edit by `build.rs` (`loadStationGarrisonPoints`' second loop names the first loop's `t`, which Visual C++ 6 allowed; it declares its own), on new stand-ins: `GameClient` (`destroyDrawable`, `findDrawableByID`), `ThingFactory::newDrawable`, the muzzle-flash drawable's calls, `Weapon::isWithinAttackRange` and `getDamageType`, the AI's victim, `Object::isMobile`, `getLastShotFiredFrame`, `Team::getControllingPlayer`, `Coord2D::toAngle` (verbatim) and a transform derived from the object's place and angle. **New cut points:** `World::contain_effect_*`, `contain_garrison_gun_exists`, `contain_current_weapon`, `contain_weapon_within_attack_range`, `contain_last_shot_fired_frame`, `contain_team_controlling_player`, `contain_set_team_opt`, `contain_drawable_set_indicator_color`, `contain_clear_and_set_model_condition_flags`, `contain_drawable_replace_model_condition_flags`, `contain_record_building_garrisoned`, `contain_set_position_z`; `ModuleCall::{AiGetCurrentVictim, AiGetCurrentVictimPos}`; `Undefined::InvalidBoolLoaded` (a load reading a byte other than 0 or 1 into a `Bool`, noted by `Xfer::xfer_bool` and recorded by `World::xfer_module_slot`). **Upstream facts kept:** a garrison point's object and effect are unions with the ids a load writes, until `loadPostProcess` finds them (`PointRef`; a pointer a load left is undefined when read through, and a save through it); a load's unknown original team throws in the middle of the xfer, so the fields after it are put back; `findConditionIndex` reads the body unchecked; `trackTargets` orients the effect of the point the rider was at; `loadStationGarrisonPoints` reads the drawable unchecked; `recalcApparentControllingPlayer` reads `*begin()` of the module's own list when the count is not 0 (undefined when the list is empty). **Harness corrections:** the Rust scene set the angle through `set_orientation`, whose `reactToTransformChange` redeployed the container's riders before the first op (the driver sets `m_orientation`): it now sets it quietly; the driver's fire-point stand-in wrote transforms only (a garrison asks for positions); the boxed point arrays keep `UpdateBody` small (the unboxed module made the deep `isPassengerAllowedToFire` recursion overflow the stack). Results: 9,000 cases, 1,862,134 op outputs, 0 mismatches; plants 1601-1636: 35 of 36 caught (1617, the apparent controller's allies test, is unobservable with the test's recorded `setTeam`: the original team is the garrison's own).

**Layer-9 crate batch R13a (2026-10-23, `p1w13-sonnet`).** The eight sabotage crates (`modules/sabotage_crate_collide.rs`: one module with a `SabotageVictim` enum, eight factory names), tested in `tests/differential_crate_sabotage.rs` against `differential/l9_sabotage_r13.inc`, which compiles the eight `.cpp` files unchanged (the two thieves inside a local `#define min`, as `Lib/BaseType.h` gives it upstream) on new stand-ins: `Radar::tryInfiltrationEvent`, `Money::withdraw`, `Energy::setPowerSabotagedTillFrame`, `Player::onPowerBrownOutChange`, `Object::setDisabledUntil`, and two Eva messages; the drop zone's `OCLUpdate` and the internet centre's `SpyVisionUpdate`s are the real modules. **New cut points:** `CutCall::{PlayerMoneyWithdraw, PlayerSetPowerSabotagedTillFrame, PlayerOnPowerBrownOutChange, ObjectSetDisabledUntil, ObjectAttemptDamage, InGameUiAddFloatingTextNamed}`, `ObjectCuts::{crate_stub_object_calls, player_objects}`, `World::{modules_with_spy_vision, player_iterate_objects}`, `ModuleCall::IsSabotageBuildingCrateCollide`, `PlayerList::friend_set_local_index`, `Thing::friend_set_template` (the test re-kinds the victim between ops). **Upstream facts kept:** each class tests the victim's death after the base `CrateCollide::isValidToExecute`, which has already refused a dead `other` (so that test is dead code); `isLocallyControlled` is true for an unowned victim when there is no local player. Results: 9,000 cases, 67,642 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 106 excluded (upstream-undefined); the first run found the Eva defect above. Plants 1641-1655: 14 of 15 caught (1646, the dead test, is unobservable). **Open:** `SalvageCrateCollide`, ~~`UnitCrateCollide`, `VeterancyCrateCollide`~~ (batch R13b, below), `ConvertToCarBombCrateCollide`, `ConvertToHijackedVehicleCrateCollide`. **CORRECTED 2026-10-23 (batch R13b):** the driver's players had no player type (the C++ stand-in has no constructor), so its `HumanOnly` comparisons read uninitialised memory; they are now human, the Rust default, and the 9,000 cases were re-run with 0 mismatches.

**Layer-9 crate batch R13b (2026-10-23, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `UnitCrateCollide` and `VeterancyCrateCollide` (`modules/{unit,veterancy}_crate_collide.rs`), tested in `tests/differential_crate_unit_veterancy.rs` against `differential/l9_crates_r13b.inc`, which compiles the two `.cpp` files unchanged. Stand-ins: a thing factory knowing `U1` and `U2` that makes bare objects; `ExperienceTracker::{isTrainable, canGainExpForLevel}` (`ExperienceTracker.cpp`'s logic) and `gainExpForLevel` (a line); `ScriptEngine::transferObjectName`; `PartitionFilterSamePlayer`; `Object::getName`; the module shim's `isSignificantlyAboveTerrain` is now `const`, as `Thing`'s is (with an asm label apart from `wrap_l9updates.cpp`'s). **New cut points:** `CutCall::{ThingFactoryNewObject, ObjectSetOrientation, ExperienceGainForLevel, PartitionIterateSamePlayerInRange, ScriptEngineTransferObjectName}`, `World::{crate_new_object, crate_set_orientation, cut_experience_can_gain_for_level, cut_experience_gain_for_level, partition_iterate_same_player_in_range}`, `ObjectSound::CrateFreeUnit`. **Upstream facts kept:** the unit crate reads the controlling player inside its loop (an unowned collector is undefined only when the count is not 0); `findPositionAround`'s answer is not checked; the veterancy crate acts only on its AI's goal and never without an AI. Results: 9,000 cases, 66,239 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 45 excluded (upstream-undefined); plants 1661-1679: 14 of 14 caught. **Open:** ~~`SalvageCrateCollide`~~ (batch R13c, below), `ConvertToCarBombCrateCollide`, `ConvertToHijackedVehicleCrateCollide`.

**Layer-9 crate batch R13c (2026-10-23, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `SalvageCrateCollide` (`modules/salvage_crate_collide.rs`), tested in `tests/differential_crate_salvage.rs` against `differential/l9_crates_r13c.inc`, which compiles `SalvageCrateCollide.cpp` unchanged (a forwarding shim `Common/Kindof.h` for its include, which only a case-insensitive file system resolves). Stand-ins: `Object::{set,clear,test}ArmorSetFlag` (`Object.cpp:1211-1226`, verbatim: the body's, unchecked), `Object::testWeaponSetFlag`, the stand-in template's kinds (`getTemplate()->isKindOf`), `PowerAcademyStats::recordSalvageCollected`, a body keeping armor flags. **New cut points:** `CutCall::PlayerAcademyRecordSalvageCollected`, `ObjectSound::CrateSalvage`. **Upstream facts kept:** the armor upgrade takes no chance; a chance of exactly 1 draws no random number; `MoneyChance` is parsed and never read; the money is random only when the bounds differ; the controlling player is read unchecked for the academy stats and for money above 0. Results: 9,000 cases, 64,969 op outputs, 0 mismatches, 228 excluded (upstream-undefined); plants 1681-1691: 11 of 11 caught. **Open:** ~~`ConvertToCarBombCrateCollide`~~ (batch R13d, below), `ConvertToHijackedVehicleCrateCollide`.

**Layer-9 crate batch R13d (2026-10-23, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `ConvertToCarBombCrateCollide` (`modules/convert_to_car_bomb_crate_collide.rs`), tested in `tests/differential_crate_car_bomb.rs` against `differential/l9_crates_r13d.inc`, which compiles the `.cpp` unchanged. **Correction to R33 (found in R34, 2026-10-24).** `emp_update.rs` gave the scan `FROM_BOUNDINGSPHERE_3D = 1` (that is `FROM_CENTER_3D`; upstream's value is 3). The R33 test could not see it: its scan line carried the number of filters, not the distance calculation. The constant is now 3, and the scan line carries the distance type (the auto heal stand-in records it in `g_ahRangeDc`). Re-run: 9,000 cases, 476,214 outputs, 0 mismatches. With the old constant put back, 300 cases give 1,001 mismatches, so the test can now catch it.

Also: `ActionHost`'s methods and the `ActionManager` functions take the host mutably (as `WeaponHost`'s since W1a), ahead of the world's `ActionHost`, whose answers are module calls. **Deviation:** the destructor's `removeAudioEvent` is not sent (no hook for a module's destruction).
