**Layer-9 batch R79 (2026-10-26, `p1w13-sonnet`).** `ActionHost` for the world (`world/action_host_world.rs`): `ActionManager`'s queries (`logic::action_manager`, verified against upstream by `tests/differential_weapon_fire.rs`) now run on the game's own objects. Each answer is the world's object state, an object-system function (`is_mobile`, `get_carrier_deck_height`, `get_shrouded_status`, the special power lookups, the partition's shroud, the terrain's water) or a module call to the owner of the interface, in module order: the body, contain, spawn, collide, dock and parking place modules, the special power and special ability modules, and the AI's supply truck and dozer interfaces. New: `ModuleCall::{SupplyTruckGetNumberBoxes, SupplyTruckIsAvailableForSupplying, DozerGetCurrentTask, DozerGetTaskTarget, SpecialAbilityGetSpecialObjectCount, SpecialAbilityGetSpecialObjectMax, SpecialAbilityFindSpecialObjectWithProducerId}`, which the AI layer's modules (`SupplyTruckAIUpdate`, `DozerAIUpdate`, `SpecialAbilityUpdate`) will answer; a probe answers none. Unit tests pin the body, the module lookups and an end-to-end query (`canGetHealedAt`) on the world.

**Layer-10 batch P1 (2026-10-26, `p1w13-sonnet`): the first of the pathfinder.** `PathNode` and `Path` (`logic/ai/path.rs`, a new `logic::ai` tree for layer 10), tested in `tests/differential_path_p1.rs`. **A new C++ build for the pathfinder:** `differential/wrap_pathfind.cpp` compiles the REAL `AIPathfind.h` and verbatim cuts of `AIPathfind.cpp` (build.rs, `OUT_DIR/pathfind_gen`; P1's is `inline Int IABS` .. `Path::computeFlightDistToGoal`) on `differential/shim_pathfind`, a copy of the locomotor build's setup whose `PreRTS.h` renames the stand-ins and every class of `AIPathfind.h` (other builds define stand-in members of `Pathfinder`, `Path` and `PathNode`); the header's own inline `getCell` and `worldToCell` run on a map the driver lays out in a block the size of a `Pathfinder` (the cells raw memory until P2 brings `PathfindCell`'s constructor; `PathfindLayer::getCell` a NULL stand-in until P3). Three things the header needs that MSVC allowed: a forward declaration of `PathfindCell` (named first by a `friend` declaration), and the build's `-ftrivial-auto-var-init=zero` (`Path::xfer` saves an uninitialised local; the port writes 0). The path's nodes are an arena linked by index; what it asks of the pathfinder and the terrain is a `PathHost` (`isLinePassable`, `isGroundPathPassable`, `getCell`, `getLayerForDestination`, the object's layer), for the pathfinder to implement once translated. Results: 20,000 programs, 615,816 operations, 62,845,640 dump words, 0 mismatches on the first run; plants 3000-3008: 9 of 9 caught. **Next in the pathfinder:** P2 `PathfindCellInfo`/`PathfindCell` (lines ~1100-1950), P3 `ZoneBlock`/`PathfindZoneManager` (~1950-3200) and `PathfindLayer` (~3200-3830), then `Pathfinder` itself (~3830-11123) in several batches.

**Layer-10 batch P2 (2026-10-26, `p1w13-sonnet`): the pathfinder's cells.** `PathfindCellInfo` and `PathfindCell` (`logic/ai/cell.rs`), tested in `tests/differential_cell_p2.rs` against the real cell code (build.rs cuts `enum { PATHFIND_CELLS_PER_FRAME` .. `inline Bool typesMatch(` into `cell_p2.inc`, with one edit: MSVC's default-int `static warn`). The cells and the info pool are a `CellStore`: arenas linked by index, the free list threaded through `path_parent` as upstream threads it through `m_pathParent`; an obstacle is `ObstacleFacts` (id, rubble, see-through), all the cell asks of the `Object`. The test generates only calls upstream defines, judged on the Rust store; the C++ driver stops a program with a marker when a planted defect makes a call undefined on its own state. Results: 20,000 programs, 1,210,531 operations, 0 mismatches after one fix (`isObstaclePresent` ignored the obstacle id); plants 3010-3020: 11 of 11 caught. **Next:** P3 `ZoneBlock`/`PathfindZoneManager` and `PathfindLayer`.

**Layer-10 batch P3 (2026-10-26, `p1w13-sonnet`): the pathfinder's zones and layers.** The zone helpers, `ZoneBlock` and `PathfindZoneManager` (`logic/ai/zone.rs`) and `PathfindLayer` (`logic/ai/layer.rs`, the struct `PathfindLayerState` since `PathfindLayer` is the layer number in `common::game_type`), tested in `tests/differential_zone_p3.rs` against `zone_p3.inc` (build.rs: `inline Bool typesMatch(` .. `Pathfinder::Pathfinder( void )`, verbatim; the build adds `-Wno-register` and the driver `MIN`). The map is the store's first cells in upstream's column order (`GroundMap`), a layer's cells a block appended to the store; the bridge, the ground's height and the wall's pieces are a `LayerHost` (stand-ins answering from a hash in the test, the real `Bridge` and terrain when the pathfinder is translated). Upstream facts kept and documented: `waterGround` tests the water BIT (rubble and a bridge's impassable cell count as water), the left and top neighbours' ladders differ, every layer (the invalid and ground ones included) takes a fresh zone. Results: 20,000 programs, 409,944 operations, 0 mismatches on the first run; plants 3030-3042: 13 of 13 caught (a first 3032 was an equivalent mutant and was replaced). **Next:** the `Pathfinder` itself (lines 3828-11123) in batches: the map's classification and the objects' footprints, the movement checks, the searches, the paths' services.

**Layer-10 batch P4 (2026-10-26, `p1w13-sonnet`): the Pathfinder's map, footprints, wall and units' cells.** `logic/ai/pathfinder.rs`: the `Pathfinder` struct (the cell store, zone manager and 16 layers, extents, wall pieces, queue) and `reset`, `newMap`/`classifyMap`/`classifyMapCell`, `classifyObjectFootprint`/`internal_classifyObjectFootprint`/`classifyFence`, the wall's pieces, `addBridge`, `updateLayer`, `changeBridgeState`, `getRadiusAndCenter` and the units' `updateGoal`/`updateAircraftGoal`/`removeGoal`/`updatePos`/`removePos`, with the header's `getCell`/`worldToCell`/`getClippedCell`. What it reads of objects is a `PfObjectFacts` (kinds, template fence, geometry, height, layers, AI cells), and the world a `PathfinderHost` (objects in the game logic's order, terrain cliffs/water/extent/bridge interactions, the AI's wall height, the splat). Tested in `tests/differential_pathfinder_p4.rs` against `pathfinder_p4.inc` (two verbatim cuts; not the constructor or destructor, which need the vtable of the still-untranslated searches, so the driver's Pathfinder is zeroed memory with its members constructed). Upstream facts kept: footprints skip the map's last row and column; `reset` frees the map's cells last first (their infos return to the pool in that order); `classifyFence`'s bounds start with an uninitialised high corner (0 in the zeroed build, as in the port); the cliff test is a BIT test (rubble and impassable cells pinch their neighbours). Results: 20,000 programs, 412,549 operations, 0 mismatches (two driver defects fixed on the way, none in the port); plants 3050-3062: 13 of 13 caught (a first 3056 was an equivalent mutant and was replaced). **Next (P5):** the movement checks (`validMovementPosition`, `checkDestination`, `checkForMovement`, the adjust and snap family) and the lines (`iterateCellsAlongLine` and its callbacks, `isLinePassable`, `isGroundPathPassable`, the view checks).

**Layer-10 batch P5 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the Pathfinder's movement checks and lines.** `logic/ai/movement.rs` (a second `impl Pathfinder`, and a `MovementHost: PathfinderHost` for what the checks ask of the world: relations, crush levels and tests, ignored obstacles, layer heights and destination layers, weapons' terrain lines, slavers, containers, the closest tall building). The line callbacks are a `LineProc` enum (upstream's function pointers and `userData`). Tested in `tests/differential_movement_p5.rs` against `movement_p5.inc` (five verbatim cuts) and `movement_p5_head.inc` (`IS_IMPASSABLE`, `TCheckMovementInfo`). To compile the real `isLinePassable` and `isGroundPathPassable`, P1's cut now calls the driver's hashed stand-ins through a one-name edit (`p1IsLinePassable`, `p1IsGroundPathPassable`; asserted counts 4 and 1 in build.rs); P1-P4 re-run unchanged. Upstream facts kept: `validMovementTerrain`'s layer-equals-`CELL_CLEAR` typo (dead code), `adjustCoordToCell`'s `double` 0.05, `clearCellForDiameter`'s inner-loop-only `break`, `isInRegionNoZ`'s strict bounds, the bridge attacker's 3 skipped cells. Results: 10,000 programs, 216,464 operations, 0 mismatches on the first run; plants 3070-3081: 12 of 12 caught (a first 3072 was an equivalent mutant and was replaced). **Next (P6):** the searches: `examineNeighboringCells`, `internalFindPath`/`findPath`, `buildActualPath`/`prependCells`, the ground and hierarchical searches, then the adjust family, `findClosestPath`, `findAttackPath`, `findSafePath`, `patchPath`, `moveAllies`, the queue.

**Layer-10 batch P6 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the A* search.** `logic/ai/search.rs`: `internalFindPath`, `checkChangeLayers`, `examineNeighboringCells` (with the straight-line `examineCellsCallback`, a mutating walk beside P5's read-only one), `buildActualPath`, `prependCells`, `clip`; `ClipLine2D` joins Line2D's other functions in `logic/world/partition/filters.rs`. `PathfinderView` makes the pathfinder P1's `PathHost`, so a search's path is optimized with the real line tests; the driver's P1 stand-ins dispatch to the real ones under `g_p1RealLines`. Tested in `tests/differential_search_p6.rs` against `search_p6.inc` (five verbatim cuts, one edit: internalFindPath's `return false` for a `Path *` is `NULL`, which clang requires); `internalFindPath` is called non-virtually. The scene builder of P5 is now shared by P5 and P6 (`pfP5Setup`/`pfP5Teardown`). Upstream facts kept: a search without an object is undefined (the dozer test dereferences it), `examineNeighboringCells`' re-pricing branch is dead (`onList` is always false there), `considerTransient` is always false, the header's position overload of `validMovementPosition` truncates (`REAL_TO_INT`). Results: 4,000 programs, 96,650 operations (41,191 searches, 5,099 paths), 0 mismatches on the first run; plants 3090-3101: 12 of 12 caught. **Next (P7):** `findPath` (`clientSafeQuickDoesPathExist`, the hierarchical search `internal_findHierarchicalPath`/`processHierarchicalCell`/`buildHierachicalPath`), the ground search (`findGroundPath`, `buildGroundPath`, `groundCellsCallback`), then `findClosestPath`, `findAttackPath`, `findSafePath`, `patchPath`, the adjust family, `moveAllies`, the queue.

**Layer-10 batch P7 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): `findPath` and the hierarchical search.** `logic/ai/hierarchical.rs`. The C++ now compiles `findPath`, the Pathfinder's key function, so the vtable is emitted in the pathfinder build: the Pathfinder is built by its real constructor (P4's cut starts at it), the virtual searches of later batches are stubs in the driver (`findClosestPath`, `findAttackPath`, `findSafePath`, `patchPath`: no path) and the Snapshot members are cut verbatim; P4-P6 re-run unchanged. Tested through P6's driver (`ref_pf_p6_run`, ops 12-19) in `tests/differential_hierarchical_p7.rs`. **Two upstream defects found** (the C++ hangs or crashes; the port reproduces them; the test keeps clear of them): a search from a bridge cell to the same cell can make the goal's parent its own child, because the search compares a ground cell's BLOCK zone number with the bridge LAYER's zone number, and `prependCells` never returns; a bridge with both ends in one zone block can put the popped cell on both lists. A bridge end past the map's last cell in its last block also reads past the map array. Results: 20,000 programs, 478,777 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3110-3118: 9 of 9 caught (three only at 20,000 cases, which is the default). **Next (P8):** the ground search (`findGroundPath`, `buildGroundPath`, `groundCellsCallback`), `findClosestPath`, `pathDestination`/`checkPathCost`/`tightenPath`, the adjust family, `findAttackPath`, `findSafePath`, `patchPath`, `moveAllies`/`getMoveAwayFromPath`, the queue, `slowDoesPathExist`, `getAircraftPath`.

**Layer-10 batch P8 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the ground search.** `logic/ai/ground.rs`: `findGroundPath`, `groundCellsCallback`, `buildGroundPath`, tested through P6's driver (op 20) in `tests/differential_ground_p8.rs` against `ground_p8.inc` (two verbatim cuts). Upstream facts kept: the goal is taken as a neighbour at the PREVIOUS neighbour's cost (a variable left from the loop; uninitialised for a first neighbour, zero in the zeroed build); `centerInCell` is false. P7's upstream hang has a second form, found here: `clip` can move one end of a search onto the other's bridge cell (the test now checks for exactly that, on the Rust pathfinder, before each search). Results: 10,000 programs, 240,573 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3120-3126: 7 of 7 caught. **Next (P9):** the adjust family (`checkForAdjust`, `adjustDestination`, `adjustTargetDestination`, `checkForPossible`, `adjustToPossibleDestination`), `pathDestination`, `tightenPath`, `checkPathCost`, `findClosestPath`.

**Layer-10 batch P9 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the destination adjustments and `findClosestPath`.** `logic/ai/adjust.rs`: `checkForAdjust` (split: `adjust_candidate`, the part a line's callback can run, then the group's `tightenPath` and `checkPathCost`), `adjustDestination`, `checkForTarget`, `adjustTargetDestination`, `checkForPossible`, `adjustToPossibleDestination`, `pathDestination`, `tightenPath` (its callback a `LineProc` variant, so the line walk stays P5's), `checkPathCost`, `findClosestPath`; tested through P6's driver (ops 21-27) in `tests/differential_adjust_p9.rs` against `adjust_p9.inc` (three verbatim cuts; the driver's `findClosestPath` stub is gone). Objects gain `aircraft` and `fs_airfield` (kinds 2's bits 2 and 3; P4-P8 pass false), the weapon's range is a hashed stand-in. Upstream facts kept: `pathDestination` writes `dest` only in a debug build, and returns false (or a cell count when out of infos); `checkForAdjust`'s callers in `pathDestination` and `tightenPathCallback` pass an uninitialised position, zero in the zeroed build. **One upstream defect found:** `pathDestination`'s nested `checkPathCost` (from `checkForAdjust` with a group) cleans the shared lists, releasing the outer search's infos, and `pathDestination` then reads its goal's indices through a null info: the C++ segfaults, the port panics, and nothing upstream calls `pathDestination`. The test ends a case there (266 of 10,000). Results: 10,000 programs, 236,849 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3130-3138: 9 of 9 caught. **Next (P10):** `findAttackPath`, `findSafePath`, `patchPath`, `moveAllies`/`getMoveAwayFromPath`/`moveAlliesAwayFromDestination`, the queue, `getAircraftPath`, `slowDoesPathExist`, the Pathfinder's crc/xfer.

**Layer-10 batch P10 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the purposeful searches.** `logic/ai/attack.rs`: `findAttackPath`, `findSafePath`, `patchPath`, `getMoveAwayFromPath`, tested through P6's driver (ops 28-31) in `tests/differential_attack_p10.rs` against `attack_p10.inc` (one cut, its 19 `return false` as `return NULL`, as P6's edit); the driver's last stubs are gone, so every virtual search of the Pathfinder is now upstream's. New stand-ins: the weapon's attack distance (hashed), the AI's idleness and locomotors (word 30's bits 5 and 6-10), `LineInRegion` (the real one, linked). Upstream facts kept: `findAttackPath` moves the unit's position half a cell when it IS centred, and its fallback to the closest cell is compiled out; `patchPath` and `getMoveAwayFromPath` add the half cell to x twice and never to y; `findSafePath` ignores `from`, `getMoveAwayFromPath` its second object. Results: 10,000 programs, 239,613 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3140-3148: 9 of 9 caught. **Next (P11):** `moveAllies`, `moveAlliesAwayFromDestination`, the queue (`queueForPath`, `processPathfindQueue`), `getAircraftPath`, `slowDoesPathExist`, the Pathfinder's crc/xfer.

**Layer-10 batch P11 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): allies, aircraft paths, the request queue, the snapshot.** `logic/ai/queue.rs`: `moveAllies`, `moveAlliesAwayFromDestination` (its line walk is P5's, now `walk_line` with a closure, so `iterate_cells_along_line` and this share one Bresenham), `getAircraftPath` (`Path::insert_after` is `PathNode::append`), `queueForPath`, `processPathfindQueue`, `slowDoesPathExist`, `impl Snapshot for Pathfinder`; tested through P6's driver (ops 32-41) in `tests/differential_queue_p11.rs` against `queue_p11.inc` (the file's `#define DEBUG_QPF` and five verbatim cuts) and P7's Snapshot members. New stand-ins: the AI's activity, its `aiMoveAwayFromUnit` (logged; the unit then moving), the building not to path around, a winged locomotor, `doPathfind`, the terrain's extent (word 30's bits 11-21). **A port bug found in P5's code:** `checkForMovement` lacked infantry's pass through infantry (`INFANTRY_MOVES_THROUGH_INFANTRY`, defined in AIPathfind.h); no earlier scene set the infantry kind (P5's test now says so). Upstream facts kept: `crc` reads `m_wallPieces[MAX_WALL_PIECES]`, one past the array, 128 times, which is `m_numWallPieces` in the class's layout; `moveAlliesAwayFromDestination` reads the AI unchecked (its callers are AIs). Results: 10,000 programs, 246,781 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3150-3158: 9 of 9 caught. Thin coverage stated in the test: `moveAllies` asking a move (38), the queue's cell budget. **Next:** the Pathfinder is complete but for its debug-only members (`doDebugIcons`, `debugShowSearch`'s drawing); then AIUpdate and the AI states, which the layer-9 modules FlightDeck, SpecialAbility and Railroad wait on.

**Layer-10 batch T1 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): TurretAI and the fire state.** `logic/ai/turret.rs` (`TurretAI`, `TurretStateMachine`, its six states), `logic/ai/fire_state.rs` (`AIAttackFireWeaponState`, `outOfWeaponRangeObject`: shared with the attack machine, generic over the machine's states as `FireAttacker`, upstream's `NotifyWeaponFiredInterface`), `logic/ai/ai_update_types.rs` (AIUpdate.h's enums). **The first state machine of the AI on `common::state_machine`:** the machine's context is a host (`TurretHost: FireHost`, the owner, its AI, weapons and the world; the World will implement it with AIUpdate), and while the machine runs the turret's own fields are lent to the host (`turret_stack`), so the states reach their turret through the context as upstream's reach it through the machine; `TurretStateMachine`'s `setState`/`clear`/`resetToDefaultState` overrides are `tsm_*` functions. Tested in `tests/differential_turret_t1.rs` against `differential/wrap_turret.cpp`: TurretAI.cpp (two verbatim cuts, one implicit `int` given its `Int`; ~~the INI parsers wait for AIUpdate's data~~ **CORRECTED 2026-10-27:** the INI parsers are verified since batch A1, below), the AIStates.cpp cuts, AIStateMachine.h's declarations and StateMachine.cpp unchanged, compiled again under `Tu*` names on new stand-ins (`differential/shim_turret`). Upstream facts kept: `TurretStateMachine::xfer` writes a version only (the machine's state is not saved); the aim nulls its enemy before the pitch, so a ground target's pitch is always adjusted. Port bug found: `isTryingToAimAtTarget` must not ask the target unless aiming. Results: 10,000 programs, 318,789 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3170-3179: 10 of 10 caught. **Next:** AIUpdate (its data, the locomotors, the path following, `doPathfind`, the turrets, mood and targeting) and the AI states, as `UpdateBody` variants over the World.

**Layer-10 batch A1 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the AI's module data.** `logic/ai/ai_update_data.rs` (`AIUpdateModuleData`: its field table, `parseTurret`, `findLocomotorTemplateVector`, `parseLocomotorSet`), `TurretAIData`'s field table and parsers in `logic/ai/turret.rs`, `ModuleDataKind::AiUpdate` in `common::module`, and `ThingTemplate::friend_get_ai_module_info` with the real `Locomotor` field (the parse environment carries the `LocomotorStore`). Upstream facts kept: the locomotor sets are shared by every copy of a template's data (upstream copies the pointer and writes through `friend_getNthData`), so here they are one `Arc<RwLock<..>>`; `parseLocomotorSet` makes the set's entry before checking it, so a refused or failed set is left present; a second `Turret` throws after the first is kept; a later AI module replaces an earlier one (`clearAiModuleInfo`). Tested in `tests/differential_thing_template.rs`: two AI test modules with the real `AIUpdateModuleData` on both sides (`AIUpdate.cpp` and `TurretAI.cpp` cut verbatim into `tt_gen` by build.rs), `Locomotor` blocks read into the Locomotor build's real `TheLocomotorStore` (`ref_loco_find_template`, `ref_loco_template_key`, `ref_loco_store_new` added to `wrap_locomotor.cpp`). Results: AI module data compared 203 + 970 times (888 locomotor sets, 310 with locomotors, 470 turrets) in 9,359 + 10,079 dumps, 0 mismatches in every comparison; plants 3180-3186 and 3188 caught. **Next:** AIUpdate itself (the locomotors, the path following, `doPathfind`, the turrets, mood and targeting) and the AI states.

**Layer-10 batch A2 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): AIUpdateInterface's state and locomotion.** `logic/ai/ai_update.rs` (`AiUpdate`: every data member of the header, the constructor, `chooseLocomotorSet`/`Explicit`, `chooseGoodLocomotorFromCurrentSet`, `setLocomotorUpgrade`, `getCurLocomotorSpeed`, `hasLocomotorForSurface`, the four `setLocomotorGoal*`, `doLocomotor`, `isDoingGroundMovement`, `isAircraftThatAdjustsDestination`, `getTreatAsAircraftForLocoDistToGoal`, `getLocomotorDistanceToGoal`, `isValidLocomotorPosition`, `destroyPath`, `friend_startingMove`/`EndingMove`/`setPath`, `setQueueForPathTime`, `wakeUpNow`, the waypoint queue, `markAsDead`, the setters), generic over `AiHost: LocomotorHost + PathHost` (the object, its sleepy update, its group, the pathfinder's cell surfaces, layer update and movement position, the partition's 3-D distance, the machine's goal position until the machine is translated, the store a locomotor is made from); `ai_update_types.rs` gains `CommandSourceType`, `GuardMode`, `AttitudeType`. The state machine, the turrets' AIs, the commands and `update` are the next batches. **A new AI build** (`differential/wrap_ai.cpp`, `differential/shim_ai`, build.rs's `ai_gen`): one translation unit with Locomotor.cpp UNCHANGED, batch P1's paths and the pathfinder's `chooseBestLocomotorForPosition` over a map of real cells, AIUpdate.cpp's members verbatim (with its `#define SLEEPY_AI`), the class declared with the header's data members verbatim; every class renamed `Ai*` so that none meets the Locomotor or pathfinder build's at link time (checked: no strong symbol of the archive is defined by another); its Locomotor blocks are read by the real INI reader through INI::load's loop in the driver (INI.cpp's table names the Locomotor build's handler). Upstream facts kept: the sleepy update's comparisons are against the RELATIVE wake frame; `doLocomotor` without a path and not waiting for one moves nothing (a `DEBUG_CRASH`); the distance to an explicit goal is "not yet implemented" (0); `blockedBy` (next batch) calls the other AI before its null check. The first runs found two harness gaps (the global data's gravity and penalty not set from the scene; `SLEEPY_AI` outside the cuts), no port bug. Results: 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3200-3214 caught. **Next:** the AI state machine (`AIStateMachine`, AIStates.cpp) with `update`, the turrets' AIs inside the AI, the commands. (The test is `tests/differential_ai_update.rs` since batch A3, which renamed `differential_ai_update_a2.rs`.)

**Layer-10 batch A3 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the AI's victim, mood, morale and state queries.** `logic/ai/ai_update.rs` gains `checkForCrateToPickup`, `setCurrentVictim`, `getCurrentVictim`, `getCurrentVictimPos`, `getAttitude`, `getAIStateType`, `ignoreObstacle`, `getIgnoredObstacleID`, `getEnterTarget`, `getMoodMatrixValue`, `getMoodMatrixActionAdjustment`, `wakeUpAndAttemptToTarget`, `resetNextMoodCheckTime`, `setNextMoodCheckTime`, `canAutoAcquireWhileStealthed`, `getNextMoodTarget`, `evaluateMoraleBonus`, `isIdle`, `isAttacking`, `isBusy`, `isClearingMines`, `isMovingAwayFrom`, `isMoving` and the header's `getNextMoodCheckTime`, `notifyCrate`, `canAutoAcquire`; `AiHost` gains the state machine's answers (until it is translated), the other objects and their AIs' `addTargeter`, the controlling player and its upgrades, the team's common target, `getAbleToAttackSpecificObject`, stealth, the container, the last damage, `TheAI`'s data, vision range and `findClosestEnemy`, the current weapon's anti mask, the horde interfaces, the weapon bonus conditions and the logic random value; `ai_update_types.rs` gains `AIStateType`, the vision factors, `findClosestEnemy`'s qualifiers and `CanAttackResult`. The AI build gains the corresponding stand-ins, verbatim enums (ai_defs.h) and ai_update_a3.inc. Upstream facts kept: `checkForCrateToPickup` clears the crate's id before looking it up, so it never finds the crate; `getNextMoodTarget` reads the controlling player unchecked when called by the AI. No port bug found. Results (`tests/differential_ai_update.rs`, A2 and A3 together): 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations (half A3's), 0 mismatches; plants 3220-3229 caught, and A2's 3200-3214 again. **Next:** the AI state machine (`AIStateMachine`) and its states, then `update`, the commands and the turrets' AIs.

**Layer-10 batch A4 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the AI's state machine, its first states, and the AI's update.** `logic/ai/ai_states.rs`: `AiState<H>` (idle, dead, wait, busy, and `Untranslated { id }` whose callbacks are the host's and whose `isIdle`/`isAttack`/`isBusy` are their classes' static answers), `define_states` (upstream's constructor order and successors), `AIStateMachine`'s overrides (`aism_set_state`, `aism_clear`, `aism_reset_to_default_state`, telling the AI), `aism_update_state_machine` and `aism_set_temporary_state` (the temporary state), the goal path, `aism_xfer`; `AiUpdateModule<H>` (the AI's state and its machine: `makeStateMachine` and `onObjectCreated`, `update`). The turret pattern: the AI's state is lent to the host (`AiStateHost::ai_stack`) while its machine runs, so the states reach it through the context; `AIStateMachine`'s own members live with the AI (`AiMachineExtras`). `ai_update.rs`'s members that ask the machine now take it (`AiMachineQuery`, implemented by `StateMachine`), replacing A2/A3's host stand-ins; `StateMachine` gains `resolve_state_id` and a public `xfer_state`. **The AI build now runs the real machine:** AIStateMachine.h unchanged, StateMachine.cpp compiled again in the archive (State and StateMachine renamed `AiSm*`), AIStates.cpp's machine members and first states cut verbatim but for the constructor's placeholders (build.rs asserts 37), AIUpdate.cpp's `update`, `onObjectCreated` and `makeStateMachine` verbatim (checked: no strong symbol of the archive is defined by another). Upstream facts kept: `xfer` appends a loaded goal path to the current one and keeps the current temporary state when the stream has none; the temporary state's frames are clamped to a minute; `AI_GET_REPAIRED` is not a state of the base machine (so `getEnterTarget`'s get-repaired branch needs a subclass's machine). No port bug found. Results (`tests/differential_ai_update.rs`, A2-A4 together): 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations (a third A4's: 51,290 updates, 36,661 `setState`, 18,306 temporary states, 7,886 saves, 1,239 loads), 0 mismatches; plants 3240-3247 caught, and A2's and A3's again but 3222 (unreachable through the base machine). **Next:** the remaining AI states (move, attack, follow-path, guard, ...), the commands (`aiDoCommand`), the AI's paths (`requestPath`, `doPathfind`, `computePath`) and collisions, the turrets inside the AI, the AI's `xfer`.

**Layer-10 batch A5 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the AI's commands.** `logic/ai/ai_commands.rs`: `AiCommandParms`, `AICommandType`, `ai_do_command` (`isAllowedToRespondToAiCommands`, then each command's `private*` member: the moves, faces and follows, the attacks, the guards with the retaliation clear first, enter/dock/exit/evacuate, hunt, wander, panic, busy, the command buttons, the move out of a unit's way with its ten-second temporary state), `set_goal_position_clipped`, `do_quick_exit`, and `aism_set_goal_team` (the machine's goal squad is `logic::world::player`'s `Squad`, saved by `squad_xfer`). The commands run on the lent AI and its running machine, so the idle state's crate pick-up and mood attack now give real commands (`AiStateHost::ai_do_command` is gone); what they hand over to is `AiStateHost`'s (the containers, the action manager, the weapon's shot count, `privateAttackPosition`'s partition search and position finder, the move-away path and the allies, the command set, the special modules). The AI build adds AI.h's `AICommandParms`/`AICommandInterface` verbatim, every command of AIUpdate.cpp verbatim, the machine's squad setters, and Squad.cpp unchanged. Two harness defects found, no port bug: the module lookups' name keys are function-local statics that outlive the test's name-generator reset (the three names are now registered first in every case, on both sides), and a goal waypoint's name had no stand-in. Upstream facts kept: an AI-sourced move of a busy AI is a temporary state; `privateCombatDrop` "should never be called" and empties the container; unhandled commands (`AICMD_FOLLOW_USER_PATH`, `AICMD_DEPLOY_ASSAULT_RETURN`) do nothing. Results: 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations (128,461 commands, 9,013 quick exits), 0 mismatches; plants 3250-3256 caught, and A4's again. **Next:** the remaining AI states, the AI's paths and collisions, the turrets inside the AI, the AI's `xfer`.

**Layer-10 batch A6 (2026-10-27, `p1w13-sonnet`): the AI's paths.** `logic/ai/ai_paths.rs`: the requests (`requestPath`, `requestAttackPath`, `requestApproachPath`, `requestSafePath`), `doPathfind` (`AiUpdateModule::do_pathfind`: the AI's turn in the pathfinder's queue), `canComputeQuickPath`, `computeQuickPath`, `computePath`, `computeAttackPath`, `setPathFromWaypoint`, `friend_setGoalObject`, `isQuickPathAvailable`. The pathfinder's searches (`PathfindServicesInterface`'s `findPath`, `findClosestPath`, `findAttackPath`, `patchPath`, `findSafePath`; `getAircraftPath`, `adjustDestination`, `adjustToPossibleDestination`, `moveAlliesAwayFromDestination`, `isAttackViewBlockedByObstacle`), the weapon's range tests, the waypoints and the bridge's attack points are `AiStateHost`'s; the searches are translated (batches P2-P11) and the World wires them. The AI build adds the members verbatim (ai_update_a6.inc) over a stand-in services object whose paths come from the hash. Upstream facts kept: `m_pathTimestamp > frame - 3` is unsigned, so in the first three frames every request waits; `doPathfind`'s `m_isApproachPath & !isDoingGroundMovement()` evaluates both sides; `computeAttackPath` keeps a stuck AI's path when re-asked within two frames. No port bug found; plant 3262 (the ultra-accurate last node) survived the first run, no locomotor being ultra-accurate (op 86 sets it, as the move states do). Results: 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3260-3264 caught. **Next:** the collisions (`blockedBy`, `processCollision`, which need a second real AI), the remaining AI states, the turrets inside the AI, the AI's `xfer`, `joinTeam`, `transferAttack`.

**Layer-10 batch A7 (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the AI's move states.** `logic/ai/ai_move_states.rs`: `MoveToData` (`AIInternalMoveToState`'s members) and the base's `onEnter`, `update`, `onExit`, `computePath` (virtual: dispatched on the variant), `startMoveSound`, `xfer`; `AIMoveToState`, `AIMoveOutOfTheWayState`, `AIMoveAndTightenState`, `AIMoveAwayFromRepulsorsState` as `AiState` variants (the machine's constructor now defines them; the C++ constructor edit leaves 33 placeholders); `isSamePosition`. `AiStateHost` gains the pathfinder's `getCell`, `snapClosestGoalPosition`, `removeGoal`, the move sounds and `TheAudio`, the formation and group speed, the goal object's height, physics, speed and direction; `pf_adjust_destination` now answers. The AI build gets its own audio stand-in (Common/GameAudio.h). Upstream facts kept: the base's constructor leaves `m_waitingForPath`, `m_tryOneMoreRepath` and `m_ambientPlayingHandle` to the pool's zeros; the repath interval is unsigned; `AIMoveToState::update` runs on after its mood's attack-move command; a missile's lead uses `0.5*delta.length()` as a double; nothing calls a state's `loadPostProcess` (the machine's is empty). No port bug found. Results: 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3270-3272 caught. **Next:** the attack states (`AIAttackState` with its attack machine: approach, pursue, aim, fire), the follow-path, wander and panic states, the remaining states, the collisions.

**Layer-10 batch A8 (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the follow-path, move-and-evacuate and move-and-delete states.** `logic/ai/ai_move_states.rs`: `AIFollowPathState` (`AI_FOLLOW_PATH`, `AI_FOLLOW_EXITPRODUCTION_PATH`), `AIMoveAndEvacuateState` (`AI_MOVE_AND_EVACUATE`, `AI_MOVE_AND_EVACUATE_AND_EXIT`) and `AIMoveAndDeleteState` as `AiState` variants over `MoveToData`, with their `onEnter`, `update`, `onExit` and `xfer` (the follow path's retry count is not saved, as upstream); `ai_commands::ai_evacuate`. `AiStateHost` gains the team's `setActive` and `TheGameLogic->destroyObject`. The C++ constructor edit now leaves 28 placeholders; the states' members are cut verbatim into `ai_states_a8.inc`, with `AIUpdate.h`'s `friend_getGoalPathPosition` and `friend_setCurrentGoalPathIndex`. The test's `setState` now often picks the five new ids, and the dump adds their members. Upstream facts kept: the evacuate state's `onExit` sets the goal position to its origin (for the delete state that follows `AI_MOVE_AND_EVACUATE_AND_EXIT`); the follow path's too-close loop skips every point within a cell of the object. No port bug found. Results: 10,000 programs, 600,000 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3280-3282 caught ~~(262 to 310 result mismatches at 3,000 cases)~~ **CORRECTED 2026-10-28 (batch A9)**: those counts came from plant runs in parallel that shared one save file and loaded each other's saves; re-run with a file per process at 10,000 cases, 3280 is caught by 15 call-log mismatches, 3281 by 3, 3282 by 91 dump mismatches. **Next:** the waypoint-path, wander and panic states; the attack states; the remaining states; the collisions.

**Layer-10 batch A9 (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the waypoint, wander and panic states.** `logic/ai/ai_waypoint_states.rs`: `AIFollowWaypointPathState` (`AI_FOLLOW_WAYPOINT_PATH_AS_TEAM`, `_AS_INDIVIDUALS`), `AIFollowWaypointPathExactState` (both exact ids), `AIWanderState`, `AIWanderInPlaceState`, `AIPanicState` as `AiState` variants (`WaypointPathData` is the follow state's members, shared by wander and panic), with `getNextWaypoint`, `hasNextWaypoint`, `calcExtraPathDistance`, `computeGoal` and each state's `onEnter`, `update`, `onExit`, `xfer`; a waypoint is named by its id. `AiStateHost` gains the waypoints' link count and any link, the pathfinder's wall test and height, the group's centre, the team's current waypoint, the skirmish test with the team as a group, and the AI's skirmish fudge; `ai_commands::ai_attack_follow_waypoint_path`. The C++ constructor edit now leaves 21 placeholders; the members are cut verbatim into `ai_states_a9.inc` (from `#define ALLOW_BACKTRACK`), with `AIUpdate.h`'s waypoint-id setters. Upstream facts kept: the as-team entry dereferences the goal waypoint unchecked, `computeGoal` off the map and the exact state the current locomotor, the as-team update the controlling player; the base and the temporary state share one instance, so a waypoint state in both is left without a current waypoint when one of them ends (the next update dereferences it); with no links `getNextWaypoint` draws nothing (`[0, -1]` is `-1`) and `getLink(-1)` is none; the panic state moves on only after a success; `onEnter` of the panic state keeps the previous offset when the locomotor does not wander. The test generates none of those dereferences (the entries become idle; `chooseLocomotorSetExplicit` in such a state becomes another op; 11 of 10,000 cases end where a locomotor would be missing), keeps the controlling player while an as-team state is in the machine, and dumps the completed waypoint. Found while planting: the test's save file was shared by parallel runs (now one per process), which had made every plant look caught (see the A8 correction above). No port bug found. Results: 10,000 programs, 599,696 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3290-3297 caught. **Next:** the attack states (`AIAttackState` and its machine, the attack-follow waypoint state), the remaining states, the collisions.

**Layer-10 batch A10a (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the attack state and its machine.** `logic/ai/ai_attack_states.rs`: `AIAttackState` for `AI_ATTACK_POSITION`, `AI_ATTACK_OBJECT`, `AI_FORCE_ATTACK_OBJECT`, `AI_ATTACK_AND_FOLLOW_OBJECT` (`onEnter`, `update`, `onExit`, `xfer`, `chooseWeapon`), `AttackStateMachine`'s definitions and conditions (`inWeaponRangeObject`, `wantToSquishTarget`, `outOfWeaponRangePosition`, `cannotPossiblyAttackObject`; `outOfWeaponRangeObject` is T1's), `AIAttackAimAtTargetState`, the fire state of T1 (`fire_state`) as the AI's (`FireAttacker` for `AiState`), `ContinueState` and `FailureState` (`AiState::Utility`), and AIUpdate's `setTurretTargetObject`, `setTurretTargetPosition`, `getTurretTurnRate`, `getWhichTurretForCurWeapon`. The attack machine is a second `StateMachine<AiState<H>>`, held by the attack state's variant; while it runs, an `AttackFrame` (the original victim's position, the attacking-object flag, the AI's machine as the mood matrix sees it, a new victim) is lent to the host. `logic/ai/ai_fire_host.rs` implements `FireHost` for every `AiStateHost` (`FireHost`'s methods now take `&mut self`, as the AI's answers log). The pursue and approach states are placeholders (ids 1000, 1001) until batch A10b; the C++ edit of `AttackStateMachine`'s constructor is asserted at 4 replacements; the AI machine's placeholders are now 17. Upstream facts kept: the machine's chase states depend on the owner's kinds when the machine is made (read once, when the AI's machine is made); `m_victimTeam` and `m_lockedWeaponOnEnter` are not saved and nothing calls the state's `loadPostProcess`; `onExit` deletes the machine, exiting its state; the base and temporary attack states share one instance, so the exit of one deletes the machine under the other (not generated). Harness defects found and fixed: the AI's own object could share an id with another object of the scene (now 32 and up); the C++ data's `m_turretsLinked` was never set; the C++ `AiData` was missing the crush flag in `getAiData`; the other objects' heights are now cached per operation on the Rust side as on the C++ side; every object shared one team (the other objects now have one of two). No port bug found. Results: 10,000 programs, 599,643 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3300-3309 caught (3305 and 3309 by one mismatch each), and all earlier AI plants re-run: caught. **Next:** A10b, the pursue and approach states; then the remaining states (attack-move, attack-follow waypoint, attack squad, attack area, hunt, guard, dock, enter, exit, face, rappel, crate), the collisions.

**Layer-10 batch A10b (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the attack machine's chase states.** `logic/ai/ai_attack_states.rs`: `canPursue`, `AIAttackApproachTargetState` and `AIAttackPursueTargetState` (`ChaseData` and the move base's `MoveToData`; their `computePath` overrides dispatched from `ai_move_states::compute_path`), now defined by `define_attack_states` (which takes `follow`); the C++ side compiles `AttackStateMachine` verbatim (no placeholder edit). `AiStateHost` gains the weapon's `isTooClose`, another object's forward speed, 2-D direction, centre position and template kinds. The chase states read the AI's machine's current state (guard retaliate, hunt) from the attack frame. Upstream facts kept: the approach state's constructor leaves its victim position, timestamp and stop flag to the pool's zeros; `-MIN_RECOMPUTE_TIME` is stored in an unsigned timestamp (the frame difference is unsigned); the follow check asks the victim's template's kinds; both chase states read the controlling player unchecked (not generated without one). Harness defects found and fixed: the idle state's update reached `getNextMoodTarget`, which reads the controlling player unchecked, without one (the C++ stand-in's `getPlayerType` tolerated a NULL player; a player is now kept while the idle state is in the machine, and the team attacks no common target without one); the forced AI update kept another op's integers. The test now runs 30,000 programs by default (66 s) so that the rarer paths are held. No port bug found. Results: 30,000 programs, 1,799,000 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3310-3317 caught but 3315 (the pursuit's 0.95 speed match, whose branch about one case in a thousand reaches: caught by 3 mismatches in one scene, by none after the next generator change), and all earlier AI plants (3240-3309) re-run at 30,000: caught. **Next:** the remaining states (attack-move, attack-follow waypoint, attack squad, attack area, hunt, guard, guard retaliate, tunnel guard, dock, enter, exit, exit instantly, face, rappel, crate), the collisions.

**Layer-10 batch A11 (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the attack-move and crate states.** `logic/ai/ai_attack_move.rs`: `AIAttackMoveStateMachine`, `AIAttackMoveToState` (`AI_ATTACK_MOVE_TO`) and `AIPickUpCrateState` (`AI_PICK_UP_CRATE`, in both machines); `ai_move_states::move_to_on_enter` and `move_to_update` (`AIMoveToState`'s, now shared). The attack-move machine is made when the AI's states are defined and entered right after (upstream: by the state's constructor, during the AI's machine's); while it runs, the AI's machine's view is the bottom attack frame, so that the attack machine inside it reads the AI's machine (its mood and state: `top_view`), and `ai->friend_setGoalObject` from inside it sets the AI's machine's goal when it returns (`ai_friend_set_goal_object`; the first run found this: the attack state's exit had cleared its immediate machine's goal). The C++ edit now leaves 15 placeholders. Upstream facts kept: `m_commandSrc` is not saved; the crate state saves its goal position twice; `forceRepath` stores `-MIN_REPATH_TIME` in an unsigned timestamp; the attack-move machine's crate state is never entered (`checkForCrateToPickup` never finds the crate). Results: 30,000 programs, 1,799,110 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3320-3327 caught; of the earlier plants, all caught but 3316 (the contact weapon's extra distance, at the edge of what the scene reaches: caught before this batch's generator change, as 3315 now is again). **Next:** the hunt, attack-area and attack-follow-waypoint states (their machines' idle states acquire targets through the AI's machine), attack squad, guard, dock, enter, exit, face, rappel; the collisions.

**Layer-10 batch A12 (2026-10-28, `p1w13-sonnet`): the hunt and attack-area states.** `logic/ai/ai_hunt_states.rs`: `AIAttackThenIdleStateMachine`, `AIHuntState` (`AI_HUNT`) and `AIAttackAreaState` (`AI_ATTACK_AREA`), one `AiState::Hunt` variant with an `area` flag; their machines are made on entry and destroyed on exit (`StateMachine::destroy`: the current state exits with `EXIT_RESET`, as upstream's destructor). `ai_attack_move::run_attack_move` is now `run_sub_machine`, for every state that holds a sub-machine. `AiHost::find_closest_enemy` takes the polygon filter; `AiStateHost` gains the player's `getUnitsShouldHunt` and the attack priority info's `getPriority`. The C++ edit now leaves 13 placeholders; the stand-ins add two attack priority infos (`setAttackInfo`, op 87) and `PartitionFilterPolygonTrigger`. Port defects found by the first runs, both fixed: `doInitIdleState` asked `isIdle` of its own machine (upstream: the AI's, `ai->isIdle()`), which differs in a sub-machine whose idle state is updated (the hunt machine's; the attack-move state never updates an idle machine, so A11 could not see it); and the AI's machine's view lent to a sub-machine was built after the sub-machine was taken out of its state, so the hunt and attack-move states were not attack states while their machines attacked (A11's "the AI's machine's view is the bottom attack frame" held, but the view's attack flag was wrong). Upstream facts kept: only the hunt state's exit releases a temporary weapon lock; the attack-area state fails without an area only at a scan. Results: 30,000 programs, 1,799,396 operations, 0 mismatches; plants 3330-3336 caught; 3337 (the lock around the machine's update) is not observable here (nothing inside sets the AI's machine's state, and `friend_setGoalObject` unlocks on purpose); earlier plants (3240-3327) re-run at 30,000: all caught but 3313 (the follow continue, caught by 2 mismatches in A10b) and 3315 (the pursuit speed match), the chase states' edge branches, which this batch's generator change moved out of reach again (3316 is caught again). **Next:** the attack-follow-waypoint states (an attack-move machine in a waypoint-path state), attack squad, guard, dock, enter, exit, face, rappel; the collisions.

**Open (found 2026-10-25 in R52):** the game's allocators zero-fill (`MemoryPool::allocateBlockImplementation` and `DynamicMemoryAllocator::allocateBytesImplementation` memset 0, and the test shim's `newInstance`, `NEW` and `MSGNEW` do the same), so a member or a module data field that no constructor sets reads 0 in the shipped game. R50 and R52 now model that. Batches R29 (`StructureCollapseUpdateModuleData::m_maxBurstDelay`) and R45 (the supply centre's grant) still treat such a field as `Undefined::UninitialisedModuleData` and exclude the cases that read it: conservative, never wrong in what they compare, but they leave those cases unverified. Not re-checked for the other uses of that variant.

**Open (reporting, found 2026-10-24 in R31):** in the differential tests built on this template, the coverage tallies of the `# Results` (updates, xfers, effect lines and the like) were incremented inside `rust_run`, before a case could be excluded as undefined upstream, so they also counted cases whose outputs are not compared. The output and mismatch counts are unaffected. R28-R31 are fixed (the tally is restored for an excluded case) and their numbers corrected in place; the other tests whose `rust_run` takes the tally (35 of them, e.g. `differential_stealth_r26.rs`, `differential_horde_r25.rs`) are **Not re-checked** and their tallies may overcount by up to the excluded fraction.
**Upstream facts kept:** `CheckpointUpdate::checkForAlliesAndEnemies` tests `m_enemyScanDelay == 0 || TRUE` (the scan runs every frame; the countdown branch is dead);
`OCLUpdate::parseFactionObjectCreationList` compares a possibly `NULL` token with `stricmp`; `FlammableUpdate` saves its status enum as raw bytes. **Open (deviation):**
`~FlammableUpdate` (`stopBurningSound`) is not sent (no hook for a module's destruction). **Gate finding:** the first full gate run on batch L+M found that
`AutoHealBehavior` was the plain-test suite's example of an untranslated module (two tests now use `SpectreGunshipUpdate`), and that the particle system manager's
symbols clashed with the weapon fixture's at link time (the module build's classes are renamed `ModTest*`; `AutoHealBehavior.h`'s parser is replaced by a copy
of `INI::parseParticleSystemTemplate` through `build.rs`, asserted twice).
**Layer-9 helper and die batch verified (2026-10-21, `p1w10-sonnet`).** ~~The helpers and dies of `p1l9-helpers` (`ObjectHelper`,
`ObjectSMCHelper`, `ObjectRepulsorHelper`, `ObjectWeaponStatusHelper`, `StatusDamageHelper`, `TempWeaponBonusHelper`,
`DestroyDie`, `KeepObjectDie`, `UpgradeDie`) are pure functions returning effects.~~ **CORRECTED 2026-10-21** - the drafts were
missing `getDisabledTypesToProcess()` (`DISABLEDMASK_ALL` on the status and weapon-bonus helpers), the second constructor wake
of three helpers, `xfer` on the two stateful helpers, and the `setWakeFrame` calls (the sleep was a return value); they were
rebuilt on the `UpdateModule` / `DieModule` bases with `HelperHost` / `DieHost` (`modules/object_helper.rs`, `modules/mod.rs`) and
verified by `tests/differential_helper_modules.rs` against the nine unchanged `.cpp` files (`differential/l9_helpers.inc`): 12,000
programs, 133,142 ops, 4,206,471 words compared, 0 mismatches; 24 planted bugs (`L9_PLANT` 30-56), all caught. Not yet wired into
the object system (the host traits are implemented by the world in layer 12).
**Layer-9 create modules wired (2026-10-21, `p1w10-sonnet`).** The seven create modules of `p1l9-creates` (verified
standalone against the unchanged `.cpp` files by `tests/differential_create_modules.rs`) are now object modules: an
`UpdateBody` variant each, `ModuleDataKind` variants for the three with data of their own, factory entries
(`new_create_base`), `new_module` arms (the four with plain `CreateModuleData` are told apart by name), `Snapshot` impls
(every one is `xferVersion(1); CreateModule::xfer`, upstream-checked), `ModuleCall::OnBuildComplete` (the loop of
`BuildAssistant`, `GameLogic`, `DozerAIUpdate`, `ProductionUpdate`, `ReplaceObjectUpgrade`) and
`ModuleCall::OnSpecialPowerCreation`. `impl CreateHost for World` (`logic/world/create_host_world.rs`) answers them from the
world's own data (player preorder flag, sciences, resource gathering manager and academy stats; the object's weapon set and
experience record; the upgrade center) and from two cut points (`CutCall::PlayerAddUpgradeComplete`, which also sets the
player's completed-upgrade mask in `cuts.player_upgrades`; `PlayerAcademyRecordUpgrade`). Tests through `World`:
`create_host_world::tests`. **Convention found and documented:** a module that holds its object's id is made with
`world.next_object_id()` (the id `new_object` is about to allocate); the upgrade modules read the id from the call.
Open: `ScienceRequired` is resolved by name at use (upstream resolves at INI read), and the weapon lock of `LockWeaponCreate`
is only observable on an object with a weapon in the slot (no test builds one yet).
**Layer-9 update modules wired (2026-10-21, `p1w10-sonnet`).** `LifetimeUpdate`, `DeletionUpdate`, `BaseRegenerateUpdate` and
`SmartBombTargetHomingUpdate` (verified standalone by `tests/differential_l9_updates.rs`) are object modules.
Pieces added: `UpdateBody` variants whose `update` is the scheduler's (the module is lent to its slot while it runs, because
`destroyObject` sends `onDelete` to every module of the object, the running one included); `UpdateBody::construct`, the
world half of a constructor (a random draw, a wake call), run by `add_module` once the module is in its object, in module
order; `World::random` (the game-logic generators) and three `ObjectGlobals` fields (`hulk_max_lifetime_override`, the base
regeneration rate and delay); `ModuleCall::OnDamage`, `SetLifetimeRange`, `SetSmartBombTargetPosition`;
`impl UpdateHost/BaseRegenHost/SmartBombHost for World` (`logic/world/update_host_world.rs`), which answers for the module
that is running (the top of `cuts.module_stack`); `ModuleDataKind` variants, factory entries (`new_update_base`) and
`new_module` arms. Each module has `unconstructed` and `construct` (its constructor, split in two; `new` is both, unchanged)
and `xfer_with` (the version, the **slot's** `UpdateModule`, the fields), because the scheduling state lives in the slot.
Host trait signatures changed to `&mut self` where the world must send a call (`max_health`, `health`,
`is_significantly_above_terrain`). Tests through `World`: `update_host_world::tests` (die frames, hulk override,
`setLifetimeRange`, regeneration with a probe body, homing, an object save and load, the factory).
**Layer-9 helpers and die modules wired (2026-10-21, `p1w10-sonnet`).** The five translated helpers are what
`Object::Object` makes (`World::make_helper_module`; `cuts.stub_helpers` is the explicit ablation, set by the differential test
of `Object.cpp`, whose C++ side stands in for them), and `DestroyDie`, `KeepObjectDie` and `UpgradeDie` are die modules:
`UpdateBody` variants (the helpers are lent to their slot while they run; a die is lent around `onDie`, because

`CutCall::AdjustModelConditionForWeaponStatus` (the weapon half of the object is layer 12's; the helper makes the call every
frame, as upstream). `ObjectHelper`/the helpers split their constructors (`unconstructed`, `construct`; `new` is both).
Found by wiring: a test that removed an object from the arena without taking its update modules off the sleepy list
(`resource_gathering_manager`) now fails loudly because the helpers are update modules; it takes them off first.
Tests through `World`: `helper_host_world::tests` (the helpers asleep, status damage, temp weapon bonus with the tint, the
special model condition, the weapon status every frame in the final phase, the dies, the factory, a save and load).

## Differential-test routes, by rule

The `differential route` column of the unit table is derived from the
measured facts by these rules (nothing in it is a per-file guess):

- **direct**: the unit has a `.cpp`; compile it unchanged with shims, as
  Phase 0 did;
- **header**: header-only; a wrapper includes it and instantiates or calls it;
- **stand-ins for n later-layer unit(s)**: the unit includes headers of later
  layers, so the C++ build needs declaration-only stand-ins (or link stubs)
  for those classes until they are translated;
- **client stand-ins that log calls**: the unit touches the client; the
  stand-ins record each call so the test compares the call log with the Rust
  event queue;
- **none**: layers C and R.

Where a comparison turns out to be impossible, the module says so and is
tested against upstream logic with hand-checked cases instead.

units (lower-case basenames) and are truncated after 12 (users after 8) with
the full count in brackets; `--json` has them all.

<!-- --- p1w2-science --- -->
## Wave 2: Science and RankInfo (`p1w2-science`)

**Translated:** `common::science` (`ScienceType`, `ScienceVec`, `ScienceInfo`,
`ScienceStore`, the `Science` block, `INI::parseScience`,
`INI::parseScienceVector`, `INI::scanScience`, `INI::parseAndTranslateLabel`,
`Xfer::xferScienceType`/`xferScienceVec`) and `logic::rank_info` (`RankInfo`,
`RankInfoStore`, the `Rank` block). Verified by `tests/differential_science.rs`
against the unchanged `Science.cpp`, `RankInfo.cpp`, `INI.cpp`, `Xfer.cpp`.

**Moved to layer 7 (take a `Player`):** `ScienceStore::playerHasPrereqsForScience`,
`playerHasRootPrereqsForScience`, `getPurchasableSciences`.

**Choices:** `TheGameText->fetch` is a `GameTextFetch` argument (display strings stay
`UnicodeString`); a prerequisite cycle made by overrides recurses for ever in
`addRootSciences` and is `Undefined::ScienceCycle`; a failed `Science` block stays in the
store (with `SCIENCE_INVALID`), a failed new `Rank` block is not added.
<!-- --- end p1w2-science --- -->
<!-- --- p1w3-thing --- -->
## Layer 5, part: Thing and SimpleObjectIterator (p1w3-thing)

Translated: `logic::thing` (`Thing.h`, `Thing.cpp`) and `logic::object_iter`
(`ObjectIter.h`, `SimpleObjectIterator.cpp`), upstream commit `0a05454d`.
Cut points: the template and its kind-of queries (`ThingTemplateView`); the
terrain and `TheGlobalData->m_gravity` (`ThingContext`); `reactToTransformChange`
(returned `TransformChange`); the bridge-aware height calculation (`_by`
closures); the object's build cost (closure of `sort`).

Results (2026-10-18): `Thing` 4,000 programs, 30,023 operations, 0 mismatches;
`SimpleObjectIterator` 6,000 programs, 124,002 operations, 0 mismatches. Planted
bugs: 19 of 19 `Thing` and 8 of 8 iterator comparisons caught.

Upstream quirk kept, not fixed: `setOrientation` on a
`KINDOF_STICK_TO_TERRAIN_SLOPE` thing caches the position from before the
terrain alignment, so `getPosition()` can disagree with the transform's
translation. The differential test pins this behaviour.
<!-- --- end p1w3-thing --- -->

<!-- --- p1w3-playertemplate --- -->
## Wave 3: PlayerTemplate (`p1w3-playertemplate`)

**Translated:** `common::player_template` (`PlayerTemplate`, `PlayerTemplateStore`, the
`PlayerTemplate` block, `parseStartMoney`, `parseProductionCostChange`,
`parseProductionTimeChange`, `parseProductionVeterancyLevel`). Verified by
`tests/differential_player_template.rs` against the unchanged `PlayerTemplate.cpp`, `INI.cpp`,
`Science.cpp`, `Money.cpp`, `Handicap.cpp`.

**Moved to layer 7 (take a `Player` or an `Object`):** none. `PlayerTemplate.cpp` includes
`Common/Player.h` and does not use it; `Player` reads the getters.

**Choices:** the five image getters return the names (`TheMappedImageCollection` is the client);
`StartMoney`'s deposit drops its cash sound and `recordIncome()` (`ThePlayerList` does not exist
yet when the templates are read, see the module docs); `findPlayerTemplate`'s fifteen
`static NameKeyType`s are kept in the store, looked up on the first call and never again, so one
store models the process; a block named like one of the old maps' twelve sub-factions updates the
base faction's template and renames it to the name as written.
<!-- --- end p1w3-playertemplate --- -->

<!-- --- p1w4-sides --- -->
## Wave 4: SidesList and the Scripts data model (`p1w4-sides`, 2026-10-18)

**Translated (layer 6):** `logic::sides_list` (`SidesList`, `SidesInfo`, `TeamsInfo`/`TeamsInfoRec`,
`BuildListInfo`; `ParseSidesDataChunk`, `WriteSidesDataChunk`, `validateSides`, `addPlayerByTemplate`,
`prepareForMP_or_Skirmish` with `ParsePlayersDataChunk`/`ParseTeamsDataChunk`, the finders, `removeSide`/`removeTeam`),
`logic::scripts` (`ScriptList`, `ScriptGroup`, `Script`, `OrCondition`, `Condition`, `ScriptAction`, `Parameter`,
`Template`; every `Parse*DataChunk` and `Write*DataChunk`, the repairs of old files, `duplicate`, `duplicateAndQualify`,
`qualify`, `updateFrom`, the list editing functions), and `formats::data_chunk_output` (the writer half of
`DataChunk.cpp`, which the Phase 0 reader left out). `SidesList.cpp` 1,168 lines + `.h` 396, `Scripts.cpp` 2,718 + `.h`
1,141.

**Cut points (listed once, as the task asks):**

1. **`ScriptEngine::init`'s tables** (layer 11): the 109 condition and 344 action templates (internal name key,
   parameter types) are the input `ScriptTemplates`. Nothing here evaluates a script.
2. **Editor text:** `getUiText`, `getUiStrings` of `Script`, `Condition`, `ScriptAction`, `Parameter`; the
   `Surfaces` and `ShakeIntensities` tables.
3. **Save games:** the `Snapshot` methods (`crc`, `xfer`, `loadPostProcess`) of `SidesList`, `BuildListInfo`,
   `ScriptList`, `ScriptGroup`, `Script`.
4. **UI to engine:** `SignalUIInteraction` and `TheShellHookNames`.
5. **INI:** `BuildListInfo::parseStructure` (the AI's `BuildList` block; `AISideBuildList` is layer 10).
6. **The file system:** the skirmish scripts file is bytes (`SidesCtx::skirmish_scripts_file`).
7. **Players and templates are names:** a side is a dictionary (`playerName`, `playerFaction`, ...), a build list entry holds
   the building's and the thing template's names, a team its owner's name. No `Player`, `Team`, `ThingTemplate` is created
   or looked up (layers 7 and 5).
8. **Memory pools and the renderer:** `newInstance`/`deleteInstance`, `BuildListInfo::m_renderObj`/`m_shadowObj`.

**Shape:** linked lists are vectors (`m_nextScript`, `m_nextGroup`, `m_nextOr`, `m_nextAndCondition`, `m_nextAction`,
`m_nextBuildList`); `m_parms` stays an array of up to 12 optional parameters with a signed count (the parse leaves slots
empty and counts negative); the three enumerations a file's `readInt` is cast to are `i32`; the process-wide state
(`ScriptList::m_curId`, `s_readLists`/`s_numInReadList`, `static_readPlayerNames`, `TheSidesList`, the name keys) is an
explicit context (`SidesCtx`, `ScriptsState`) that tests keep across cases, as the original keeps it. `userData` of the
nested `parse` calls is the object last added to the list being read.

**Differential test:** `tests/differential_sides_scripts.rs` (methodology in its header). `SidesList.cpp` (one mechanical edit
by `build.rs` in a copy, asserted to match once: the call that opens the skirmish scripts file) and `Scripts.cpp`
(unchanged) are compiled with the Phase 0 `DataChunk.cpp` and `NameKeyGenerator.cpp` under `differential/shim_sides/`, driven by
`differential/wrap_sides.cpp`. Result (2026-10-18, `cargo test --release --features differential --test
differential_sides_scripts`, seed fixed): **8,000 synthetic files generated** (6,000 `SidesList` files, 2,000 `PlayerScriptsList`
files), **7,014 compared, 0 mismatches**, 129.7 MB of log (dumps of every field before and after up to 8 operations, the bytes
written, the group counter), 18.1 MB of written chunk files compared byte for byte. The parse succeeded in 6,525 files,
stopped (returned false) in 262 and threw in 227 (`ERROR_BUG` 171, an unknown `KIND_OF` name, `ERROR_CORRUPT_FILE_FORMAT`
55, `ERROR_OUT_OF_MEMORY` 1); 981 files were **undefined upstream** and not given to the C++ (`ParameterIndexOutOfRange` 474,
`UninitialisedRead` 158, `NullParameter` 104, `ActionTypeOutOfRange` 85, `ConditionTypeOutOfRange` 46, `NameTooLong` 42,
`ParameterTypeOutOfRange` 35, `KindOfIndexOutOfRange` 24, and 13 with a string holding a 0, see below), 5 had a table of
contents declaring more than 10^5 names. What the compared files held: 15,267 sides, 20,076 build list entries, 18,583
script lists, 46,639 scripts, 17,995 groups, 67,834 conditions (11,749 made false by a bad count or an unknown name), 114,615
actions (19,537 made no-ops), 275,851 parameters; each of the 26 operations ran 165 to 713 times; `prepareForMP_or_Skirmish` read
the standard scripts file 100 times and gave scripts to a skirmish side 40 times. The constants (92: the enumerators the
translation names, `NUM_ITEMS` of the three tables, `MAX_PARMS`, `KINDOF_COUNT`, `OBJECT_STATUS_COUNT`) are compared with
the C++ by `constants_match_upstream`.

**Planted bugs** (a deliberate error in the translation, one at a time, to see the comparison fail; run on 2,000 files
generated by the same seed (`SIDES_CASES0=1500 SIDES_CASES1=500`, 1,734 compared), all removed afterwards):

| planted | files that caught it |
|---|---|
| build entry `z` not forced to 0 (parse field) | 928 |
| `TEAM_FOLLOW_WAYPOINTS` repair defaults its flag to 0 (old-file repair) | 224 |
| `whiner`/`unsellable` written in the other order (written bytes) | 816 |
| no `validateSides` after the write (state after the write) | 297 |
| `ScriptGroup::duplicate` takes no number of the group counter (counter) | 1,731 |
| allies/enemies keep the player itself (`validateSides`) | 510 |
| `qualify` of a `SIDE` compares the unqualified name | 9 |
| `static_readPlayerNames` not cleared after the skirmish file | 3 |
| `health` read as its absolute value | 211 |
| type tag in `keyAndType` off by one (writer) | 1,573 |
| `CRUSHER` read to index 1 instead of the current index | 23 |
| `Fundamentalist` replaced by `Gla` | 549 |
| version 1 condition repair adds `SURFACES_ALLOWED` 2 instead of 3 | 219 |
| `addScript` with a negative position goes to the front | 43 |
| `WriteParameter` leaves the `KIND_OF` string | 76 |
| leftover read lists not dropped by the next `ParseScriptsDataChunk` | 3 |
| a constant (`PERCENT` 50 instead of 51) | 1 of 92 |

(Two other plants, reading the build entry's extra fields only from version 4 and leaving the count of the version 1 condition
repair at 2, made the Rust choose operations the C++ then ran into undefined behaviour with: the test process died with
SIGSEGV. They are not in the table.)

**Which numbers to quote:** the 7,014 compared files and 0 mismatches; the 981 undefined files are not a result about the
translation (the Rust refuses them, the C++ would read through a null or out-of-bounds pointer or an unspecified enum).

**Upstream behaviour found and kept (all pinned by the test):**

- `PlayerScriptsList` is written with version **5**: `K_SCRIPTS_DATA_VERSION_1` follows `K_SCRIPT_CONDITION_VERSION_4 = 4`
  in an enum.
- A condition or action whose name key matches no template becomes `CONDITION_FALSE` / `NO_OP`, but its parameters are read
  by the count in the file and compared with the **last** template's count, because the search loop leaves `ct`/`at` there.
- The version 1 repair `m_parms[m_numParms] = SURFACES_ALLOWED; m_numParms = 3` sets the count to 3, not one more; with a
  count of 0 or 1 it leaves empty slots below the count (writing such a condition dereferences `NULL`).
- `KIND_OF_PARAM` `MISSILE`: the retry loop compares the string with the literal it was just set to, so it stops at index 0
  and the outer loop goes on from 1 until it meets `SMALL_MISSILE`'s real index; `CRUSHER` and the others take the current
  index, which is 0 (the check is made on the first pass).
- `ParseSidesDataChunk` breaks out of its loop at 16 sides but not out of the file: the unread sides' bytes are read as the
  teams and scripts that follow. `ParseScriptsDataChunk` returns false for a 17th script list, which `ParseSidesDataChunk`
  turns into `ERROR_CORRUPT_FILE_FORMAT`; the previous read lists are gone but their count stays.
- `ParseScript` returns `NULL` if its children fail to parse, and both callers store the result.
- `WriteSidesDataChunk` runs `validateSides` after the chunk is closed (it can change the list the caller still holds);
  `WriteParameter` replaces the string of a `KIND_OF_PARAM`.
- `SidesInfo::operator=` copies the script list with `ScriptList::duplicate`, which takes a number of the group counter per
  group, so `removeSide` and `prepareForMP_or_Skirmish` advance the counter; `operator=` on itself does nothing.
- `validateSides` restarts its team-name check with a `goto` after `removeTeam`, which refuses to remove the last team: a
  list with one team named like a player would loop forever (unreachable after the default teams are made; the Rust returns
  `MapUndefined::NonTermination`).
- `Condition::duplicate` after the first condition of a chain copies as many parameters as the original has, from slots
  that exist only up to the template's count: a condition made false by an unknown name keeps the file's count and its
  copy dereferences `NULL`.

**Harness record.** New: `differential/shim_sides/` (stand-ins for `PlayerTemplate.h`, `AI.h`, `Radar.h`, `ThingTemplate.h`,
`ContainModule.h`, `View.h`, `Player.h`, `Object.h`, `NetworkDefs.h`, `KindOf.h`, `GameState.h` with `SaveCode`, `ScriptEngine.h`
with the two tables; each says what it stands in for), `differential/wrap_sides.cpp`, one `build.rs` block (the lib is
compiled ahead of `upstream_chunk`, `-fno-delete-null-pointer-checks` because upstream calls `deleteInstance()` on `NULL`).
Added to shared shims, each in a `p1w4-sides` block: `AsciiString::startsWith` and `nextToken(tok)`,
`UnicodeString::translate(AsciiString)`, `Dict::clear`/`getBool`/`getAsciiString` of the chunk build's stand-in, the declaration of
`KindOfMaskType::getBitNames` (defined in `wrap_sides.cpp` over the real name table). `wrap_chunk.cpp`'s
`getPath_UserData` stand-in, which aborted, calls a hook (`ref_chunk_user_data_path_hook`) that `wrap_sides.cpp` installs, so
`DataChunkOutput` can write its temporary file. **Limit of the stand-in:** the `std::string` `AsciiString` of this build counts
bytes past an embedded 0 in `getLength()` where the engine's `strlen`s, so a file with a string holding a 0 would make the C++
write more than the engine would; `DataChunkInput::embedded_nul_strings` (added, additive) counts such strings and the test
leaves those files out (13 of 8,000).
Knobs of the test: `SIDES_CASES0`, `SIDES_CASES1` (files of each kind), `SIDES_NOOPS` (no operations),
`SIDES_DUMP_DIR` (write the logs of a mismatching file there).
<!-- --- end p1w4-sides --- -->
<!-- --- p1w4-terrain --- -->
## Layer 6, part: TerrainLogic (p1w4-terrain)

Translated: `logic::terrain_logic` (`TerrainLogic.h`, `TerrainLogic.cpp`: the
map-chunk parse of waypoints, links, triggers and bridges, height-map ground
queries, bridges and their layers, water areas and water changes over time,
waypoints and paths, flatten and crater, `xfer`), `logic::terrain_visual` (the
logic-owned state of what `TheTerrainVisual` is read for, and the client
events for what it is written), and the petir-side helpers `ww::plane` and
`ww::tri`. Upstream commit `0a05454d`.

**Corrected count.** ~~`TerrainLogic` calls `TheTerrainVisual` 32 times, with
`setRawMapHeight` 20~~ **CORRECTED 2026-10-18**: 32 calls in total
(`grep -c TheTerrainVisual`), of which `setRawMapHeight` is **19** (the 20th
grep hit is a comment at line 2717). The three reads are as the paragraph above
says.

**Water grid and raw heights (recorded, NOT decided).** The Rust holds both as
logic state: `WaterGrid` (transform, resolution, mesh heights, clamps) and
`LogicHeightMap` (raw heights, lowered only by `setRawMapHeight`). Where
upstream reads them: `getWaterGridHeight` 2164 and 2222, `getWaterTransform`
2316 and 2515, `getWaterGridResolution` 2502, `getRawMapHeight` 2899. What the
client would otherwise supply: the mesh vertex heights, which
`WaterRenderObjClass::update()` simulates **per client frame** (only on a
client frame whose logic frame differs from the last seen), so `isUnderwater`
over a grid would depend on the client's frame rate. That simulation is not
translated; heights are set by the embedder or stay 0. Undecided: whether the
grid becomes simulated logic state or is shown irrelevant. Not checked: whether
any shipped map has a `WaveGuide1` waypoint and is in
`m_vertexWaterAvailableMaps` (the map files are not here). Until then a grid
water map is not lockstep-safe. A fourth read lives outside `TerrainLogic.cpp`
(`W3DTerrainLogic` ground height through the render object, which reads the
same logic height map; translated). `isCliffCell` and `isClearLineOfSight` are
not translated.

**Cut points.** Writes to `TheTerrainVisual` are `TerrainVisualEvent`s (load,
enable grid, height clamps, transform, resolution, attenuation, raw height).
Calls into other layers go through the `TerrainWorld` trait (no trait object;
a type parameter): object lookup and position/orientation/layer reads, bridge
tower and landmark objects (`ThingFactory::newObject`, `ThingTemplate`
lookups), `TheAI->pathfinder()` (layer changes, wall height), the partition
manager (`unRegisterObject`/`registerObject` of bridge objects), body-module
damage and repair queries, `TheGameLogic->getFrame()`, and the boundary-change
notification. `PolygonTrigger` and `MapObject` are minimal stand-ins (`TriggerArea`,
`WaypointMapObject`) to be replaced when the sibling `PolygonTrigger`/`MapObject`
work lands.

**Choices.** Lists are `Vec`s in upstream order (waypoints walked in reverse,
bridges and triggers head first). Undefined-upstream inputs are `Err` and are
counted (`undefined_inputs`): an uninitialised `pickBridge` position, a stale
water handle, a missing water transform, and 11 others.

**Upstream quirks kept and pinned:** the `update()` copy loop that removes a
finished water entry (copies entry `j` over `i` for every `j`); `setGridResolution`
comparing `m_gridCellsY != m_gridCellsY`; `flatten` and `crater` loops starting
`j` at 0; the `tri.h` degenerate case; the swapped comments of `isCellOnEnd` and
`isCellOnSide`.

**Results (2026-10-18, `tests/differential_terrain_logic.rs`, 8 tests):** 24,000
programs, 22,139 compared, 346,279 operations, 0 mismatches in results, the
world log or the client-event log; floats bitwise. 11 planted bugs: 10 caught
(two only after the generator was strengthened), 1 not caught (a 0.001 bias in
a tie-break). Table in the test's module doc.
<!-- --- end p1w4-terrain --- -->
